# NxTerra structure loot inventory

Baseline labels describe the shipped table contents; these are editorial labels, not configured gameplay tiers. Rare-capable and Epic-capable mark possible rewards, not their odds. Item lists show explicit entries; tags, nested tables, hard-coded generators, global modifiers, chance, conditions and Lootr behavior can change actual rewards.

Scope includes all statically linked loot table IDs, all chest, archaeology and vault definitions, and recursively referenced tables. Unlinked definitions are retained for investigation. More than one source can declare an ID. Optional built-in definitions are labelled individually. The winning runtime source for duplicate mod definitions needs confirmation.

**960** table IDs inventoried. **652** directly linked from structure definitions or reachable jigsaw pools. **764** referenced by container templates, including alternatives not statically linked to a registry structure.
